{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787310872480,"sessionID":"ses_fdbf89db7ffeR4cz150MGhNuDA","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"skill","callID":"call-1dd4df9b-a9d2-4d66-8b9b-51c1cf2508cd-0","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"name":"fastapi-python"},"output":"<skill_content name=\"fastapi-python\">\n# Skill: fastapi-python\n\n# FastAPI Python\n\nYou are an expert in FastAPI and Python backend development.\n\n## Key Principles\n\n- Write concise, technical responses with accurate Python examples\n- Favor functional, declarative programming over class-based approaches\n- Prioritize modularization to eliminate code duplication\n- Use descriptive variable names with auxiliary verbs (e.g., `is_active`, `has_permission`)\n- Employ lowercase with underscores for file/directory naming (e.g., `routers/user_routes.py`)\n- Export routes and utilities explicitly\n- Follow the RORO (Receive an Object, Return an Object) pattern\n\n## Python/FastAPI Standards\n\n- Use `def` for pure functions, `async def` for asynchronous operations\n- Use type hints for all function signatures. Prefer Pydantic models over raw dictionaries\n- Structure: exported router, sub-routes, utilities, static content, types (models, schemas)\n- Omit curly braces for single-line conditionals\n- Write concise one-line conditional syntax\n\n## Error Handling\n\n- Handle edge cases at function entry points\n- Employ early returns for error conditions\n- Place happy path logic last\n- Avoid unnecessary else statements; use if-return patterns\n- Implement guard clauses for preconditions\n- Provide proper error logging and user-friendly messaging\n\n## FastAPI-Specific Guidelines\n\n- Use functional components (plain functions) and Pydantic models for input validation\n- Declare routes with clear return type annotations\n- Prefer lifespan context managers for managing startup and shutdown events\n- Leverage middleware for logging, error monitoring, and optimization\n- Use HTTPException for expected errors and model them as specific HTTP responses\n- Apply Pydantic's BaseModel consistently for validation\n\n## Performance Optimization\n\n- Minimize blocking I/O; use async for all database and API calls\n- Implement caching with Redis or in-memory stores\n- Optimize Pydantic serialization/deserialization\n- Use lazy loading for large datasets\n\n## Key Conventions\n\n1. Rely on FastAPI's dependency injection system\n2. Prioritize API performance metrics (response time, latency, throughput)\n3. </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { CustomerProject, ProjectPageContentItem, ProjectListMetrics } from '~/types/api'\nimport { metricLabel } from '~/utils/ranking'\nimport { collectionIsClosed, projectRemainingTimeCopy } from '~/utils/projectEndTime'\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst snackbar = useSnackbar()\nconst api = useProjectsApi()\nconst votesApi = useVotesApi()\nconst { requireAdmin, isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled } = useAuth()\nconst runningAi = ref(false)\n\nasync function runAiBaseline() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n  if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n    snackbar.error('Save at least two options before running AI agents')\n    return\n  }\n  runningAi.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votesApi.runAiBaseline(project.value.id)\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    if (res.already_complete) snackbar.success('AI agents have already finished')\n    else snackbar.success('AI agents started. Results appear when they finish.')\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not start AI agents')\n  }\n  finally {\n    runningAi.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nconst loading = ref(true)\nconst project = ref<CustomerProject | null>(null)\nconst alternatives = ref<ProjectPageContentItem[]>([])\nconst factors = ref<ProjectPageContentItem[]>([])\nconst myObservationCount = ref(0)\nconst participantCount = ref(0)\nconst targetComparisonsEst = ref(0)\nconst maxComparisonsEst = ref(0)\nconst activeParticipantCount = ref(0)\nconst myMetrics = ref<ProjectListMetrics>({})\n\nconst activeAlts = computed(() => alternatives.value.filter(a => !a.disabled))\nconst activeFactors = computed(() => factors.value.filter(f => !f.disabled))\nconst remainingTimeCopy = computed(() => projectRemainingTimeCopy(project.value?.end_time))\nconst inputClosed = computed(() => !!project.value?.disabled || collectionIsClosed(project.value?.end_time))\nconst canRunAiAgents = computed(() =>\n  !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n  && isAdmin.value\n  && !!project.value?.include_ai_agents\n  && (project.value?.ai_voter_models || []).length > 0,\n)\n\nconst nextAction = computed(() => {\n  const id = project.value?.id\n  if (!id) return { title: '', detail: '', label: '', to: '/projects' }\n  if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Complete project setup',\n      detail: 'Add at least two realistic options before collecting input.',\n      label: 'Continue setup',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/edit`,\n    }\n  }\n  if (inputClosed.value) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Review current results',\n      detail: 'Comparison collection is closed. Results remain available.',\n      label: 'View results',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/results`,\n    }\n  }\n  if (myObservationCount.value === 0 && participantCount.value === 0) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Begin collecting input',\n      detail: 'Invite people or start comparing yourself.',\n      label: 'Start comparing',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/probe`,\n    }\n  }\n  return {\n    title: 'Review current results',\n    detail: `Confidence is ${metricLabel(Number(myMetrics.value.confidence ?? 0))}. Open results to explain the outcome.`,\n    label: 'View results',\n    to: `/projects/${id}/results`,\n  }\n})\n\nfunction makeDuplicate() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n  navigateTo(`/projects/0/edit?from=${project.value.id}`)\n}\n\nonMounted(async () => {\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await api.getProjectPageSummary(Number(route.params.id))\n    if (res.failure_reason || !res.project) throw new Error(res.failure_reason || 'Project not found')\n    project.value = res.project\n    alternatives.value = res.alternatives || []\n    factors.value = res.factors || []\n    myObservationCount.value = res.my_observation_count || 0\n    participantCount.value = res.participant_count || 0\n    targetComparisonsEst.value = res.target_comparisons_est || 0\n    maxComparisonsEst.value = res.max_comparisons_est || 0\n    activeParticipantCount.value = res.active_participant_count || 0\n    myMetrics.value = res.my_metrics || {}\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load project')\n  }\n  finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n})\n</script>\n\n<style scoped>\n@media (max-width: 960px) {\n  .summary-hero {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;\n  }\n}\n</style>","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":269,"totalLines":269,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/projects/[id]/index.vue","time":{"start":1787310878607,"end":1787310879648}},"id":"prt_02407ae4e0012WQW4IL8y0ZyU0","sessionID":"ses_fdbf89db7ffeR4cz150MGhNuDA","messageID":"msg_02407a2b6001CHaX4QVPNq8TaX"}}

alternatives=alts,\n        factors=factors,\n        exclusive_mode=bool(project.project_exclusive_mode),\n        settings=ranking_settings,\n        participants=[part] if part is not None else create_shared_link_record(self, session: Session, usr_context: UserContext, clear_lock: bool=True) -> ShareLinkResult_One:\n        self.id = None\n        self.magic_token = None\n        if usr_context is not None and usr_context.sess_user is not None and (usr_context.sess_user.id != self.user_id or usr_context.sess_user.customer_id != self.customer_id):\n            log_event(\"Unauthorized attempt to create shared link\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=3,\n                      raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=\"Unauthorized attempt to create shared link for another user/account\"))\n\n        if self.shared_type in get_share_object_actions():\n            hooks = get_hooks()\n            if raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, detail=\"Share link support is not fully configured\"))\n            resolved = hooks.entity_resolver(session=session, usr_context=usr_context, entity_id=self.shared_entity_db_id)\n            if resolved is None or resolved.customer_id != usr_context.sess_user.customer_id:\n                log_event(\"Associated entity not found or not current customer\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=3,\n                        raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, detail=\"Cannot access entity specified attempting create a shared link\"))\n            if self.share_link_name in (None, \"\"):\n                self.share_link_name = resolved.display_name\n        else:\n            log_event(\"Requesting unknown or unspecified share type\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=4,\n                      raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, detail=f\"Requesting unknown share type {self.shared_type} - {self.shared_entity_db_id}\"))\n\n        if self.share_actions is None:\n            self.share_actions = list(get_hooks().share_action_matrix.get(self.shared_type, [])) or None\n        else:\n            unknown_actions = [a for a in self.share_actions if a not in get_share_access_operations()]\n            if unknown_actions:\n                log_event(f\"Requesting share with unknown actions {unknown_actions}\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=4,\n                          raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, detail=f\"Unknown share actions {unknown_actions} for share request {self.shared_type}\"))\n\n        if self.access_mode in (ShareAccessCheckMode.not_specified,):\n            log_event(\"Requesting share without access_mode setting\", usr_context=usr_context, severity=4,\n                      raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, detail=f\"Access Mode not set {self.access_mode} for share request {self.shared_type} - {self.shared_entity_db_id}\"))\n        self.magic_token = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(uuid.uuid4().bytes).decode(\"ascii\").strip(\"=\")\n        if self.share_password is not None and len(self.share_password):\n            self.share_password = get_hex_hash_from_args(settings.SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY, self.magic_token, self.share_password, settings.SHARE_LINK_REST_PW_HASH_KEY)\n        session.add(self)\n        session.commit()\n        session.refresh(self)\n        if clear_lock: session.close()\n        return ShareLinkResult_One(link_info=self)\n\n    def update_shared_link_record(self, session: Session, usr_context: UserContext, clear_lock: bool=True) -> ShareLinkResult_One:\n        if usr_context.sess_user.id != self.user_id or usr_context.sess_user.customer_id != self.customer_id:\n            log_event(\"Unauthorized attempt to update shared link for another user/customer\", ShareLinkResult_One(failure_reason=existing_row_result.failure_reason, log_id=existing_row_result.log_id)\n        if existing_row_result.link_info is None:\n            session.close()\n raise_exception=HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=\"Unauthorized attempt to update shared link for another customer\"))\n        session.add(self)\n        session.commit()\n get_shared_link_by_id_or_magic_token_system(session: Session, usr_context: UserContext | None=None, shared_link_id: int | None=None, shared_magic_token: str | None=None, clear_lock: bool=True, allow_disabled_link_retrieval: bool = False) -> ShareLinkResult_One:\n        share_link_info: ShareLink | None = None\n        if shared_link_id is not None:\n            share_link_info = session.exec(select(ShareLink).where(ShareLink.id == shared_link_id).limit(1)).one_or_none()\n            if share_link_info:\n                shared_magic_token = share_link_info.magic_token\n        elif shared_magic_token is not None:\n            share_link_info = session.exec(select(ShareLink).where(ShareLink.magic_token == shared_magic_token).limit(1)).one_or_none()\n            if share_link_info:\n                shared_link_id = share_link_info.id\n        else:\n            log_event(\"Invalid request for shared link, no id or magic key was provided\", severity=3,\n                      usr_context=usr_context, raise_exception=True)\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=\"Invalid shared link request\")\n        if share_link_info is None:\n            session.close()\n            return ShareLinkResult_One(failure_reason=f'Shared link {shared_magic_token}/{shared_link_id} was not found')\n        if share_link_info.share_link_enabled is False and allow_disabled_link_retrieval is False:\n            session.close()\n            return ShareLinkResult_One(failure_reason=f'Shared link {shared_magic_token}/{shared_link_id} is not enabled')\n        if share_link_info.share_link_expiration >= 0 and share_link_info.create_date + timedelta(days=share_link_info.share_link_expiration) < datetime.now():\n            session.close()\n            return ShareLinkResult_One(failure_reason=f'Shared link {shared_magic_token}/{shared_link_id} has expired')\n        if clear_lock: session.close()\n        return ShareLinkResult_One(link_info=share_link_info)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_shared_links_by_customer_id_and_project_ids(session: Session, usr_context: UserContext, project_ids: list[int] | None=None, clear_lock: bool=True) -> ShareLinkResult_Many:\n        customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n        if project_ids is None:\n            share_link_info_list = session.exec(select(ShareLink).where(ShareLink.customer_id == customer_id)).all()\n        else:\n            share_link_info_list = session.exec(select(ShareLink).where(ShareLink.customer_id == customer_id, ShareLink.shared_entity_db_id.in_(project_ids))).all()\n        if clear_lock: session.close()\n        return ShareLinkResult_Many(link_info_list=share_link_info_list)\n\n\n# The actual Field(default=None, index=True, description=\"The associated share event id\")\n    share_magic_token: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"The visible token value for the share\")\n create_date: datetime = Field(sa_column=Column(DateTime, default=func.now()))\n    modify_date: datetime = Field(sa_column=Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now()))\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def create_shared_magic_key_record(session: Session, usr_context: UserContext,\n                                       share_link_id: int,\n              original_link_recipient_email: str|None=None,\n                                       clear_lock: bool=True) -> Union[ShareLinkMagicKey, None]:\n        magic_record = ShareLinkMagicKey(share_id=share_link_id)\n        magic_record.share_magic_token = shared_magic_token\n        magic_record.access_magic_key = get_random_baseN_value(\n            length=6, digits_only=settings.token_security_digits_only\n        )\n        magic_record.share_token_and_access_magic_key = f\"{shared_magic_token}:{magic_record.access_magic_key}\"\n        magic_record.captured_email = captured_email\n        magic_record.original_link_recipient_email = original_link_recipient_email\n        magic_record.captured_display_name = captured_display_name\n        session.add(magic_record)\n        session.commit()\n        session.refresh(magic_record)\n        if clear_lock: session.close()\n        return magic_record\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def session.close()\n        return share_magic_keys\n\nclass ShareLinkAccessed(SQLModel, table=True):\n    id: int | None = Field(default=None, primary_key=True)\n    share_id: int | None = _complete_one_group(client, project_id):\n    mine = client.get(\"/ws/project-votes/my-vote\", params={\"project_id\": project_id, \"request_next\": True})\n    assert mine.status_code == 200, = []\n    for i in range(len(rank) - 1):\n        pairings.append({\n            \"winner_id\": rank[i],\n            \"loser_id\": rank[i + 1],\n            \"response\": \"winner\",\n            \"decision_seconds\": 0.1,\n            \"presented_left_id\": rank[i],\n            \"presented_right_id\": rank[i + 1],\n        })\n    res = client.post(\n        \"/ws/project-votes/complete-group\",\n        json={\n            \"project_id\": project_id,\n            \"prior_group\": {\n                \"client_group_id\": g[\"client_group_id\"],\n    },\n        },\n    )\n    assert res.status_code == 200, res.text\n    assert not res.json().get(\"failure_reason\"), res.json()\n    return res\n\n\ndef test_project_create_as_admin(client, account):\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    tag = uid(\"proj\")\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/ws/custprojects/customer-project-create\",\n        json={\n            \"project_tag\": tag,\n            \"project_title\": \"Title\",\n            \"project_description\": \"desc\",\n            \"disabled\": False,\n        },\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body.get(\"failure_reason\", \"\") in (\"\", None)\n    project = body.get(\"customer_project_info\") or {}\n    if project.get(\"id\"):\n        account.tracked_project_ids.append(project[\"id\"])\n        account.tracked_app_ids.append(project[\"id\"])\n        account.project_tag = tag\n        assert project.get(\"project_created_by\") == {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787311189351,"sessionID":"ses_fdbf89db7ffeR4cz150MGhNuDA","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call-668399c2-8e7b-4611-9467-6f0e1d3d407b-71","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/project_overview_participation.py","content":"from __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, select\n\nfrom ..db.models.ai_agent_jobs import AiAgentJob\nfrom ..db.models.project_vote_events import ProjectVoteParticipant, VoteSource\nfrom ..pvf.bindings.pvf_services import ShareLink, ShareLinkMagicKey\nfrom ..pvf.db.models.email_activity_log import EmailActivityLog\nfrom .ai.config import normalize_model_key\nfrom .ai.identity import ai_display_name, build_ai_participant_key, is_ai_participant\n\n\n_VOTE_SHARE_TYPES = frozenset({\"vote\", \"vote_view\"})\n_LOCKED_EMAIL_MODES = frozenset({\n    \"email_matching\",\n    \"email_matching_verified\",\n    \"recipient_email_verified\",\n    \"password_with_email_matching\",\n    \"password_with_email_matching_verified\",\n    \"password_with_recipient_email_verified\",\n})\n_OPEN_VERIFIED_MODES = frozenset({\n    \"email_any_verified\",\n    \"password_with_email_any_verified\",\n})\n_ANONYMOUS_MODES = frozenset({\n    \"open_access\",\n    \"email_any_unverified\",\n    \"password_only\",\n    \"password_with_email_any_unverified\",\n})\n_SHARE_INVITE_EMAIL_TYPES = (\n    \"share_project_vote\",\n    \"share_project_vote_view\",\n    \"share_project_report\",\n    \"resending_invitation\",\n)\n_IN_FLIGHT_STATUSES = frozenset({\"queued\", \"in_progress\"})\n\n\nclass AiAgentCounts(BaseModel):\n    requested: int = 0\n    in_flight: int = 0\n    completed: int = 0\n\n\nclass HumanParticipantActiveItem(BaseModel):\n    label: str\n    comparison_count: int = 0\n    participant_id: int | None = None\n\n\nclass HumanParticipantPendingItem(BaseModel):\n    label: str\n    days_pending: int = 0\n    email: str | None = None\n    participant_id: int | None = None\n    share_id: int | None = None\n\n\nclass AnonymousShareItem(BaseModel):\n    label: str\n    activated: int = 0\n    share_id: int | None = None\n\n\nclass HumanParticipantsSummary(BaseModel):\n    active: list[HumanParticipantActiveItem] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    pending: list[HumanParticipantPendingItem] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    anonymous_shares: list[AnonymousShareItem] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\nclass AiParticipantStatusItem(BaseModel):\n    model_key: str = \"\"\n    display_name: str = \"\"\n    status: str = \"\"\n    pairs_answered: int = 0\n    pairs_remaining: int = 0\n\n\ndef _mode_value(share: Any) -> str:\n    mode = getattr(share, \"access_mode\", None)\n    return str(getattr(mode, \"value\", mode) or \"\")\n\n\ndef _share_type(share: Any) -> str:\n    raw = getattr(share, \"shared_type\", None)\n    return str(getattr(raw, \"value\", raw) or \"\")\n\n\ndef _naive_dt(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:\n    if value is None:\n        return None\n    if getattr(value, \"tzinfo\", None) is not None:\n        return value.replace(tzinfo=None)\n    return value\n\n\ndef _days_since(value: datetime | None) -> int:\n    ts = _naive_dt(value)\n    if ts is None:\n        return 0\n    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)\n    seconds = (now - ts).total_seconds()\n    return max(0, int(seconds // 86400))\n\n\ndef _norm_email(value: str | None) -> str:\n    return (value or \"\").strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _label(*parts: str | None) -> str:\n    for part in parts:\n        text = (part or \"\").strip()\n        if text:\n            return text\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef _invite_sent_by_token(session: Session, customer_id: int, shares: list) -> dict[str, datetime]:\n    emails = {\n        _norm_email(getattr(share, \"shared_with_email\", None))\n        for share in shares\n        if _norm_email(getattr(share, \"shared_with_email\", None))\n    }\n    tokens = {share.magic_token for share in shares if getattr(share, \"magic_token\", None)}\n    if not emails or not tokens:\n        return {}\n    rows = session.exec(\n        select(EmailActivityLog)\n        .where(\n            EmailActivityLog.customer_id == customer_id,\n            EmailActivityLog.email_address.in_(list(emails)),\n            EmailActivityLog.email_type.in_(_SHARE_INVITE_EMAIL_TYPES),\n        )\n        .order_by(EmailActivityLog.id.desc())\n    ).all()\n    latest: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n    for row in rows:\n        token = (row.email_params_json or {}).get(\"magic_token\")\n        if token in tokens and token not in latest:\n            latest[str(token)] = row.create_date\n    return latest\n\n\ndef _vote_shares(shares: list) -> list:\n    return [s for s in shares if _share_type(s) in _VOTE_SHARE_TYPES]\n\n\ndef _parts_for_share(humans: list[ProjectVoteParticipant], share_id: int) -> list[ProjectVoteParticipant]:\n    return [p for p in humans if int(getattr(p, \"share_id\", 0) or 0) == int(share_id)]\n\n\ndef _active_count(parts: list[ProjectVoteParticipant]) -> int:\n    return sum(1 for p in parts if int(p.comparison_count or 0) >= 1)\n\n\ndef build_human_participants(\n    session: Session,\n    *,\n    customer_id: int,\n    project_id: int,\n    humans: list[ProjectVoteParticipant],\n) -> HumanParticipantsSummary:\n    shares = list(session.exec(\n        select(ShareLink).where(\n            ShareLink.customer_id == customer_id,\n            ShareLink.shared_entity_db_id == project_id,\n        )\n    ).all())\n    vote_shares = _vote_shares(shares)\n    share_ids = [int(s.id) for s in vote_shares if s.id is not None]\n    magic_keys = list(session.exec(\n        select(ShareLinkMagicKey).where(ShareLinkMagicKey.share_id.in_(share_ids))\n    ).all()) if share_ids else []\n    keys_by_share: dict[int, list] = {}\n    for key in magic_keys:\n        sid = int(getattr(key, \"share_id\", 0) or 0)\n        keys_by_share.setdefault(sid, []).append(key)\n    invite_by_token = _invite_sent_by_token(session, customer_id, vote_shares)\n\n    active: list[HumanParticipantActiveItem] = []\n    pending: list[HumanParticipantPendingItem] = []\n    anonymous: list[AnonymousShareItem] = []\n    claimed_active: set[int] = set()\n    claimed_pending_shares: set[int] = set()\n    claimed_pending_emails: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()\n\n    for part in humans:\n        if part.source == VoteSource.session and int(part.comparison_count or 0) >= 1:\n            active.append(HumanParticipantActiveItem(\n                label=_label(part.display_name, part.email, \"Team member\"),\n                comparison_count=int(part.comparison_count or 0),\n                participant_id=part.id,\n            ))\n            if part.id is not None:\n                claimed_active.add(int(part.id))\n\n    for share in vote_shares:\n        mode = _mode_value(share)\n        sid = int(share.id)\n        parts = _parts_for_share(humans, sid)\n        if mode in _LOCKED_EMAIL_MODES or mode in _OPEN_VERIFIED_MODES:\n            for part in parts:\n                if int(part.comparison_count or 0) < 1:\n                    continue\n                if part.id is not None and int(part.id) in claimed_active:\n                    continue\n                active.append(HumanParticipantActiveItem(\n                    label=_label(part.display_name, part.email, share.shared_with_person_name, share.shared_with_email, \"Participant\"),\n                    comparison_count=int(part.comparison_count or 0),\n                    participant_id=part.id,\n                ))\n                if part.id is not None:\n                    claimed_active.add(int(part.id))\n                    email = _norm_email(part.email)\n                    if email:\n                        claimed_pending_emails.add((sid, email))\n\n    for part in humans:\n        if part.source != VoteSource.session:\n            continue\n        if int(part.comparison_count or 0) >= 1:\n            continue\n        pending.append(HumanParticipantPendingItem(\n            label=_label(part.display_name, part.email, \"Team member\"),\n            days_pending=_days_since(part.create_date),\n            email=part.email,\n            participant_id=part.id,\n        ))\n\n    for share in vote_shares:\n        mode = _mode_value(share)\n        sid = int(share.id)\n        token = getattr(share, \"magic_token\", None)\n        invite_at = invite_by_token.get(token) if token else None\n        parts = _parts_for_share(humans, sid)\n        if mode in _LOCKED_EMAIL_MODES:\n            if _active_count(parts) >= 1:\n                continue\n            zero_part = next((p for p in parts if int(p.comparison_count or 0) == 0), None)\n            pending.append(HumanParticipantPendingItem(\n                label=_label(\n                    getattr(zero_part, \"display_name\", None) if zero_part else None,\n                    getattr(zero_part, \"email\", None) if zero_part else None,\n                    share.shared_with_person_name,\n                    share.shared_with_email,\n                    share.share_link_name,\n                    \"Invited participant\",\n                ),\n                days_pending=_days_since(invite_at or share.create_date),\n                email=_label(getattr(zero_part, \"email\", None) if zero_part else None, share.shared_with_email) or None,\n                participant_id=zero_part.id if zero_part is not None else None,\n                share_id=sid,\n            ))\n            claimed_pending_shares.add(sid)\n            continue\n        if mode in _OPEN_VERIFIED_MODES:\n            for key in keys_by_share.get(sid, []):\n                if getattr(key, \"accessed_date\", None) is None:\n                    continue\n                email = _norm_email(key.captured_email)\n                if email and (sid, email) in claimed_pending_emails:\n                    continue\n                matched = None\n                for part in parts:\n                    if email and _norm_email(part.email) == email:\n                        matched = part\n                        break\n                if matched is not None and int(matched.comparison_count or 0) >= 1:\n                    continue\n                pending.append(HumanParticipantPendingItem(\n                    label=_label(\n                        key.captured_display_name,\n                        key.captured_email,\n                        getattr(matched, \"display_name\", None) if matched else None,\n                        getattr(matched, \"email\", None) if matched else None,\n                        \"Verified participant\",\n                    ),\n                    days_pending=_days_since(key.accessed_date or invite_at or share.create_date),\n                    email=key.captured_email or (matched.email if matched is not None else None),\n                    participant_id=matched.id if matched is not None else None,\n                    share_id=sid,\n                ))\n                if email:\n                    claimed_pending_emails.add((sid, email))\n            continue\n        if mode in _ANONYMOUS_MODES:\n            anonymous.append(AnonymousShareItem(\n                label=_label(share.share_link_name, share.shared_with_person_name, \"Open share\"),\n                activated=_active_count(parts),\n                share_id=sid,\n            ))\n\n    active.sort(key=lambda row: (row.label.lower(), row.participant_id or 0))\n    pending.sort(key=lambda row: (-row.days_pending, row.label.lower()))\n    anonymous.sort(key=lambda row: (row.label.lower(), row.share_id or 0))\n    return HumanParticipantsSummary(active=active, pending=pending, anonymous_shares=anonymous)\n\n\ndef _latest_jobs_by_model(jobs: list[AiAgentJob]) -> dict[str, AiAgentJob]:\n    latest: dict[str, AiAgentJob] = {}\n    for job in jobs:\n        key = normalize_model_key(getattr(job, \"model_key\", None))\n        if key not in latest:\n            latest[key] = job\n    return latest\n\n\ndef _ai_part_for_model(parts: list[ProjectVoteParticipant], model_key: str) -> ProjectVoteParticipant | None:\n    want_key = build_ai_participant_key(model_key)\n    want_model = normalize_model_key(model_key)\n    keyed = None\n    modeled = None\n    for part in parts:\n        if str(getattr(part, \"participant_key\", \"\") or \"\") == want_key:\n            keyed = part\n            break\n        if normalize_model_key(getattr(part, \"ai_model\", None)) == want_model:\n            modeled = modeled or part\n    return keyed or modeled\n\n\ndef _job_status(job: AiAgentJob | None, *, participant_complete: bool) -> str:\n    if participant_complete:\n        return \"complete\"\n    if job is None:\n        return \"complete\" if participant_complete else \"queued\"\n    status = str(job.status or \"\")\n    if status == \"complete\" or participant_complete:\n        return \"complete\"\n    if status in (\"queued\", \"in_progress\", \"failed\"):\n        return status\n    return status or \"queued\"\n\n\ndef _pairs_answered(job: AiAgentJob | None, part: ProjectVoteParticipant | None) -> int:\n    if job is not None:\n        pairs = int(job.pair_count or 0)\n        if pairs > 0:\n            return pairs\n    if part is not None:\n        return int(part.comparison_count or 0)\n    return 0\n\n\ndef build_ai_participation(\n    session: Session,\n    *,\n    customer_id: int,\n    project_id: int,\n    participants: list[ProjectVoteParticipant],\n    target_comparisons_est: int,\n) -> tuple[AiAgentCounts, list[AiParticipantStatusItem]]:\n    jobs = AiAgentJob.list_for_project(\n        session=session,\n        customer_id=customer_id,\n        project_id=project_id,\n        clear_lock=False,\n    )\n    latest = _latest_jobs_by_model(jobs)\n    ai_parts = [p for p in participants if is_ai_participant(p)]\n    model_keys: list[str] = []\n    seen: set[str] = set()\n    for key in latest:\n        if key not in seen:\n            seen.add(key)\n            model_keys.append(key)\n    for part in ai_parts:\n        key = normalize_model_key(getattr(part, \"ai_model\", None))\n        if key not in seen:\n            seen.add(key)\n            model_keys.append(key)\n\n    rows: list[AiParticipantStatusItem] = []\n    in_flight = 0\n    completed = 0\n    for model_key in model_keys:\n        job = latest.get(model_key)\n        part = _ai_part_for_model(ai_parts, model_key)\n        participant_complete = bool(part and part.is_complete)\n        status = _job_status(job, participant_complete=participant_complete)\n        if status in _IN_FLIGHT_STATUSES:\n            in_flight += 1\n        elif status == \"complete\":\n            completed += 1\n        pairs = _pairs_answered(job, part)\n        remaining = 0 if status == \"complete\" else max(0, int(target_comparisons_est or 0) - pairs)\n        details = getattr(job, \"details_json\", None) or {}\n        display = (\n            (details.get(\"display_name\") if isinstance(details, dict) else None)\n            or getattr(part, \"display_name\", None)\n            or ai_display_name(model_key)\n        )\n        rows.append(AiParticipantStatusItem(\n            model_key=model_key,\n            display_name=str(display),\n            status=status,\n            pairs_answered=pairs,\n            pairs_remaining=remaining,\n        ))\n    rows.sort(key=lambda row: (row.display_name.lower(), row.model_key))\n    counts = AiAgentCounts(\n        requested=len(model_keys),\n        in_flight=in_flight,\n        completed=completed,\n    )\n    return counts, rows\n"},"output":"Wrote file assert public[\"activated\"] == 1\n    assert \"Report Only\" not in pending_labels\n    assert \"Report share\" not in anon_labels\n    assert \"AI AGENT: glm-5.2\" not in active_labels\n    assert create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=title)\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    body = _page_summary(client, project.id)\n    target = session.add(AiAgentJob(\n        customer_id=account.customer.id,\n        project_id=project.id,\n        job_type=\"ai_baseline\",\n        model_key=\"grok-4.6\",\n        status=\"queued\",\n session.add(ProjectVoteParticipant(\n        customer_id=account.customer.id,\n        project_id=project.id,\n        participant_key=\"ai:glm-5.2\",\n        display_name=\"AI AGENT: glm-5.2\",\n
